const { chromium } = require('playwright');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const MASTER_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'Master_Controller');
const INPUT_SELECTOR = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'ai_selectors.json'))).gemini.chatBox; } catch(e) { return 'rich-textarea, textarea, div[role="textbox"][contenteditable="true"], .ql-editor'; } })();

// UI à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦¨à¦¾à¦® à¦°à¦¿à¦¸à¦¿à¦­ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ (à¦¡à¦¿à¦«à¦²à§à¦Ÿ: Gemini_Profile)
const profileName = process.argv[2] || 'Normal_Browser';
const ACCOUNTS_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'Accounts', profileName);

if (!fs.existsSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR, { recursive: true });

let browser, context, page;
let isRunning = false;
let waitForUserPromise = null; // ðŸ”´ à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦…à¦¨à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦•à¦¾à¦² à¦“à§Ÿà§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦®à¦¿à¦¸

// ðŸ”´ Helper: main.js à¦à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦§à§à¦¯à¦®à§‡ UI-à¦¤à§‡ à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦­ à¦•à¦¨à¦¸à§‹à¦² à¦²à¦— à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹
function sendLog(type, text) {
    if (process.send) process.send({ type: 'console', logType: type, text: text });
    else console.log(`[${type.toUpperCase()}] ${text}`);
}

// ðŸ”´ Helper: à¦ªà§à¦°à¦—à§à¦°à§‡à¦¸ à¦¬à¦¾à¦° à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦¸ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹
function sendStatus(state, data = {}) {
    if (process.send) process.send({ type: 'status', state, ...data });
}

const randomDelay = (min, max) => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.random() * (max - min) + min));

async function startAutomation(prompts) {
    if (isRunning) return;
    isRunning = true;

    try {
        sendLog('info', `ðŸš€ Launching Gemini Image Automation on Profile: [${profileName}]`);
        sendLog('info', `ðŸ“Š Total Prompts Received: ${prompts.length}`);

// --- ðŸ”´ à¦¡à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿ à¦“ à¦¹à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¿à¦¡ à¦•à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨ à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦• ---
        const PORTS_FILE = path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'active_ports.json');
        let targetPort = 9222;
        
        try {
            if (fs.existsSync(PORTS_FILE)) {
                let portsData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(PORTS_FILE, 'utf8'));
                if (portsData[profileName]) {
                    targetPort = portsData[profileName]; 
                } else {
                    const usedPorts = Object.values(portsData);
                    targetPort = usedPorts.length > 0 ? Math.max(...usedPorts) + 1 : 9222;
                    portsData[profileName] = targetPort;
                    fs.writeFileSync(PORTS_FILE, JSON.stringify(portsData, null, 4));
                }
            } else {
                fs.writeFileSync(PORTS_FILE, JSON.stringify({ [profileName]: 9222 }, null, 4));
            }
        } catch(e) {}

        const endpoint = `http://127.0.0.1:${targetPort}`;
        
        try {
            sendLog('connect', `Checking for existing Browser on Port ${targetPort}...`);
            browser = await chromium.connectOverCDP(endpoint);
            context = browser.contexts()[0];
            page = await context.newPage();
            sendLog('connect', `âœ… Connected to existing browser session!`);
        } catch (cdpError) {
            sendLog('connect', `Launching new browser window...`);
            context = await chromium.launchPersistentContext(ACCOUNTS_DIR, {
                channel: 'chrome',
                headless: false,
                viewport: null,
                args: [
                    `--remote-debugging-port=${targetPort}`,
                    '--start-maximized', 
                    '--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled'
                ]
            });
            page = context.pages()[0] || (await context.newPage());
        }
        // ---------------------------------------------------------

        page.setDefaultTimeout(0);

        sendLog('connect', 'Navigating to Gemini...');
        await page.goto('https://gemini.google.com/app', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });

        // ==========================================================
        // ðŸ”¥ MAGIC FIX: à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦…à¦¨à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦•à¦¾à¦² à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡
        // ==========================================================
        sendLog('waiting', 'ðŸ›‘ ACTION REQUIRED: à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡ à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦šà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿ à¦¸à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦•à§à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤ à¦°à§‡à¦¡à¦¿ à¦¹à¦²à§‡ à¦¡à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶à¦¬à§‹à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ "Resume" à¦¬à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¨à§‡ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨!');
        sendStatus('paused'); // UI à¦•à§‡ à¦¬à¦²à§‡ à¦¦à¦¿à¦²à¦¾à¦® à¦¯à§‡ à¦¬à¦Ÿ à¦ªà¦œ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡ à¦†à¦›à§‡
        
        // à¦¯à¦¤à¦•à§à¦·à¦£ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° Resume à¦¬à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¨à§‡ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡, à¦•à§‹à¦¡ à¦à¦–à¦¾à¦¨à§‡ à¦†à¦Ÿà¦•à§‡ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦¬à§‡
        await new Promise(resolve => { waitForUserPromise = resolve; });
        
        sendLog('ready', 'â–¶ï¸ Resume signal received! Starting automation...');
        sendStatus('running');

        let sessionPromptCount = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < prompts.length; i++) {
            if (!isRunning) break; 

            const currentPrompt = prompts[i];
            
            sendLog('progress', `--- ðŸŽ¨ Generating Image (Prompt ${i + 1} / ${prompts.length}) ---`);
            sendStatus('prompt', { index: i, text: currentPrompt });

// à¦‡à¦¨à¦ªà§à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦²à§‹à¦¡ à¦¹à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦ªà¦°à§à¦¯à¦¨à§à¦¤ à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦•à¦°à¦¾
            const promptBox = page.locator(INPUT_SELECTOR).last(); // .first() à¦à¦° à¦¬à¦¦à¦²à§‡ .last() à¦¹à¦¬à§‡
            await promptBox.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 0 });

            await promptBox.click({ force: true });
            await page.waitForTimeout(300);
            
            // à¦•à¦¿à¦¬à§‹à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦¦à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡ à¦¸à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦•à§à¦Ÿ à¦…à¦² à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¡à¦¿à¦²à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾
            await page.keyboard.press('Control+A');
            await page.keyboard.press('Backspace');
            await randomDelay(300, 600);

            sendLog('typing', `Typing prompt...`);
            
            // à¦¸à§à¦ªà¦¾à¦° à¦«à¦¾à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦ªà¦¿-à¦ªà§‡à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦œà§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨ (à¦¯à§‡à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡ à¦•à¦¾à¦œ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡)
            await page.evaluate((text) => {
                const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
                textarea.value = text;
                document.body.appendChild(textarea);
                textarea.select();
                document.execCommand('copy');
                document.body.removeChild(textarea);
            }, currentPrompt);

            await promptBox.click({ force: true });
            await page.keyboard.press('Control+V');
            await randomDelay(800, 1500);

            await page.keyboard.press('Enter');

            // à¦œà§‡à¦®à¦¿à¦¨à¦¿à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¸à§‡à¦¸à¦¿à¦‚-à¦à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿ à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦•à¦°à¦¾
            sendLog('generating', `[PROMPT ${i + 1}] Waiting for image generation to complete...`);
            await randomDelay(3000, 5000); 
            
            // à¦œà§‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦¹à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦ªà¦°à§à¦¯à¦¨à§à¦¤ à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ (Stop à¦¬à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¨ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾ à¦—à§‡à¦²à§‡ à¦¬à§à¦à¦¬ à¦œà§‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦¹à¦šà§à¦›à§‡)
            const stopButtonSelector = 'button[aria-label*="Stop"]';
            const isGenerating = await page.locator(stopButtonSelector).count();
            if (isGenerating > 0) {
                await page.waitForSelector(stopButtonSelector, { state: 'hidden', timeout: 0 });
            }
            
            sendLog('completed', `ðŸŽ‰ Image generated successfully!`);
            await randomDelay(4000, 6000); 

            // UI à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦—à§à¦°à§‡à¦¸ à¦¬à¦¾à¦° à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹
            sendStatus('progress', { completed: i + 1, total: prompts.length });

            // à¦…à¦Ÿà§‹-à¦°à¦¿à¦«à§à¦°à§‡à¦¶ à¦²à¦œà¦¿à¦• (à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à¦¿ à§®à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦®à§à¦ªà¦Ÿ à¦ªà¦° à¦ªà¦°)
            sessionPromptCount++;
            if (sessionPromptCount >= 8) {
                sendLog('info', `[ðŸ”„ AUTO-REFRESH] Refreshing page to clear RAM/Memory...`);
                await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
                await randomDelay(8000, 12000); 
                sessionPromptCount = 0; 
            }
        }

        sendLog('ready', 'âœ… Automation Complete! All images generated.');
        sendStatus('done');
        isRunning = false;

    } catch (error) {
        sendLog('error', `Engine Crashed: ${error.message}`);
        isRunning = false;
    }
}

// ðŸ”´ Master Controller à¦à¦° à¦¸à¦¿à¦—à¦¨à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦² à¦°à¦¿à¦¸à¦¿à¦­ à¦•à¦°à¦¾
process.on('message', async (msg) => {
    if (msg.type === 'start') {
        startAutomation(msg.prompts);
    } else if (msg.type === 'resume') {
        // UI à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ Resume à¦¸à¦¿à¦—à¦¨à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦² à¦ªà§‡à¦²à§‡ à¦¬à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦†à¦Ÿà¦•à§‡ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦®à¦¿à¦¸ à¦¸à¦²à¦­ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¦à§‡à¦¬à§‡
        if (waitForUserPromise) {
            waitForUserPromise();
            waitForUserPromise = null;
        }
    } else if (msg === 'shutdown' || msg.type === 'stop') {
        isRunning = false;
        sendLog('info', 'Shutting down engine...');
        try { if (page) await page.close(); if (context) await context.close(); } catch(e){}
        process.exit(0);
    }
});

/* === grok_engine.js === */

const { chromium } = require('playwright');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// ðŸ”´ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦¨à¦¾à¦® à¦°à¦¿à¦¸à¦¿à¦­ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦•à§‹à¦¡ 
const profileName = process.argv[2] || 'Normal_Browser';

// --- Master Controller Paths ---
const MASTER_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'Master_Controller');
const INBOX_FILE = path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'Inbox', 'grok_inbox.json');
const OUTBOX_DIR = path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'Outbox');

// ðŸ”´ à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦• à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§à¦¸ à§§: à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦«à§‹à¦²à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦° à¦ªà¦¾à¦¥
const ACCOUNTS_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'Accounts', profileName); 

if (!fs.existsSync(OUTBOX_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(OUTBOX_DIR, { recursive: true });
if (!fs.existsSync(path.dirname(INBOX_FILE))) fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(INBOX_FILE), { recursive: true });
if (!fs.existsSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR, { recursive: true }); // à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦«à§‹à¦²à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦° à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿

const State = {
    INITIATING: 'Initiating System',
    CONVERSATION_READY: 'Conversation Ready',
    LISTENING: 'Listening for Tasks',
    TYPING: 'Typing',
    PROMPT_SENT: 'Prompt Sent',
    GENERATING: 'Generating',
    COMPLETED: 'Completed',
    OUTPUT_COPIED: 'Output Copied',
    OUTPUT_SAVED: 'Output Saved'
};

let currentState = State.INITIATING;
let browser, context, page;
let currentTask = null; 
let currentOutput = "";
let initialCopyCount = 0; 

// ðŸ”´ à¦†à¦²à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦®à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦¸à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦•à§à¦Ÿà¦°: à¦²à§‡à¦–à¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à¦‡ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à§à¦• à¦¨à¦¾ à¦•à§‡à¦¨, à¦¸à§‡ à¦¶à§à¦§à§ à¦¸à§à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§‡à¦° "à¦¦à§ƒà¦¶à§à¦¯à¦®à¦¾à¦¨" (Visible) à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à§‡à¦‡ à¦§à¦°à¦¬à§‡
const INPUT_SELECTOR = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'ai_selectors.json'))).grok.chatBox; } catch(e) { return 'textarea:visible, [contenteditable="true"]:visible'; } })();
const randomDelay = (min, max) => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.random() * (max - min) + min));

(async () => {
    console.log(`\n======================================================`);
    console.log(` ðŸŒŒ Grok AI Worker Node [Profile: ${profileName}]`);
    console.log(`======================================================\n`);

    while (true) { 
        switch (currentState) {
            
            // ðŸ”´ 100% FIXED: SMART HYBRID MODE + ANTI-BOT BYPASS + DYNAMIC PORT
            case State.INITIATING:
                try {
                    // --- ðŸ”´ à¦†à¦²à¦¾à¦¦à¦¾ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦†à¦²à¦¾à¦¦à¦¾ à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿ à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦• ---
                    const PORTS_FILE = path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'active_ports.json');
                    let targetPort = 9222;
                    
                    try {
                        if (fs.existsSync(PORTS_FILE)) {
                            let portsData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(PORTS_FILE, 'utf8'));
                            if (portsData[profileName]) {
                                targetPort = portsData[profileName]; 
                            } else {
                                const usedPorts = Object.values(portsData);
                                targetPort = usedPorts.length > 0 ? Math.max(...usedPorts) + 1 : 9222;
                                portsData[profileName] = targetPort;
                                fs.writeFileSync(PORTS_FILE, JSON.stringify(portsData, null, 4));
                            }
                        } else {
                            fs.writeFileSync(PORTS_FILE, JSON.stringify({ [profileName]: 9222 }, null, 4));
                        }
                    } catch(e) {}

                    const endpoint = `http://127.0.0.1:${targetPort}`;
                    
                    try {
                        // à§§. à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¥à¦®à§‡ à¦šà§‡à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿ ${targetPort} à¦¤à§‡ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¨ à¦†à¦›à§‡ à¦•à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾
                        console.log(`[STATE] ðŸŒ Checking for Browser on Port ${targetPort} (Profile: ${profileName})...`);
                        browser = await chromium.connectOverCDP(endpoint);
                        context = browser.contexts()[0];
                        page = await context.newPage();
                        console.log(`[INFO] âœ… Connected! Opening Grok in a new tab...`);
                    } catch (cdpError) {
                        // à§¨. à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à§‡, à¦¤à¦¬à§‡ à¦¸à§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦‡ à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦‰à¦‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à§‹ à¦–à§à¦²à¦¬à§‡!
                        console.log(`[INFO] Browser not found. Launching new window for ${profileName}...`);
                        const ACCOUNTS_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'Accounts', profileName); 
                        if (!fs.existsSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR, { recursive: true });
                        
                        context = await chromium.launchPersistentContext(ACCOUNTS_DIR, {
                            headless: false,
                            channel: 'chrome',
                            viewport: null,
                            ignoreDefaultArgs: ["--enable-automation"], 
                            args: [
                                `--remote-debugging-port=${targetPort}`, 
                                '--start-maximized',
                                '--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled',
                                '--no-sandbox',
                                '--disable-infobars'
                            ]
                        });
                        let pages = context.pages();
                        page = pages.length > 0 ? pages[0] : await context.newPage();
                        console.log(`[INFO] âœ… New Browser Launched successfully on port ${targetPort}!`);
                    }

                    page.setDefaultTimeout(0); 
                    await page.goto('https://grok.com/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
                    
                    await page.waitForTimeout(5000); 
                    currentState = State.CONVERSATION_READY;
                } catch (err) {
                    console.log(`[ERROR DETAILS] ${err.message}`); 
                    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 5000));
                }
                break;

            case State.CONVERSATION_READY: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸ’¬ ${currentState}: Handling popups and finding input box...`);
                await page.evaluate(() => {
                    document.querySelectorAll('div[role="dialog"], [class*="cookie"], [class*="banner"]').forEach(e => e.remove());
                });
                
                // ðŸ”´ à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§à¦¸: à¦²à§à¦•à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸ à¦‡à¦—à¦¨à§‹à¦° à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¶à§à¦§à§ Visible à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯à¦‡ à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡
                await page.locator(INPUT_SELECTOR).last().waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 0 });
                currentState = State.LISTENING; 
                break;

            case State.LISTENING:
                await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
                try {
                    if (fs.existsSync(INBOX_FILE)) {
                        const rawData = fs.readFileSync(INBOX_FILE, 'utf8');
                        if (rawData.trim() !== "" && rawData.trim() !== "{}") {
                            const data = JSON.parse(rawData);
                            if (data.prompt && data.task_id) {
                                console.log(`\n[STATE] ðŸ“¥ NEW TASK RECEIVED from ${data.sender}! Task ID: ${data.task_id}`);
                                currentTask = data;
                                currentState = State.TYPING;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e) {}
                break;

            case State.TYPING: 
                console.log(`[STATE] âŒ¨ï¸  ${currentState}: Blazing fast Copy-Paste typing...`);
                
                // ðŸ”´ à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§à¦¸: à¦¸à§à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦à¦•à¦¦à¦® à¦¸à¦¾à¦®à¦¨à§‡à¦° Visible à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡
                const promptBox = page.locator(INPUT_SELECTOR).last();
                await promptBox.click({ force: true });
                await page.waitForTimeout(300);
                
                await page.keyboard.press('Control+A');
                await page.keyboard.press('Backspace');
                await randomDelay(100, 200);
                
                await page.evaluate((text) => {
                    const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
                    textarea.value = text;
                    document.body.appendChild(textarea);
                    textarea.select();
                    document.execCommand('copy');
                    document.body.removeChild(textarea);
                }, currentTask.prompt);

                await promptBox.click({ force: true });
                await page.keyboard.press('Control+V'); 
                
                await randomDelay(800, 1500);
                currentState = State.PROMPT_SENT;
                break;

            case State.PROMPT_SENT: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸš€ ${currentState}: Pressing 'Enter' to submit.`);
                initialCopyCount = await page.evaluate(() => {
                    return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button')).filter(b => 
                        (b.getAttribute('aria-label') || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy') || 
                        (b.title || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy')
                    ).length;
                });

                await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
                currentState = State.GENERATING;
                break;

            case State.GENERATING: 
                console.log(`[STATE] âš™ï¸  ${currentState}: AI is generating. Monitoring DOM...`);
                try {
                    await page.waitForFunction((initial) => {
                        const current = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button')).filter(b => 
                            (b.getAttribute('aria-label') || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy') || 
                            (b.title || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy')
                        ).length;
                        return current > initial;
                    }, initialCopyCount, { timeout: 120000 });

                    console.log(`[INFO] New AI message detected! Checking text stability (waiting for stream to stop)...`);

                    let lastText = "";
                    let stableChecks = 0;
                    
                    while (stableChecks < 2) {
                        await page.waitForTimeout(1500); 
                        
                        const currentText = await page.evaluate(() => {
                            const btns = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button')).filter(b => 
                                (b.getAttribute('aria-label') || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy') || 
                                (b.title || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy')
                            );
                            if(btns.length === 0) return "";
                            
                            let container = btns[btns.length - 1].parentElement;
                            for(let i=0; i<4; i++) { if(container.parentElement && container.parentElement.tagName !== 'BODY') container = container.parentElement; }
                            return container.innerText;
                        });

                        if (currentText === lastText && currentText.length > 5) {
                            stableChecks++;
                        } else {
                            stableChecks = 0;
                            lastText = currentText;
                        }
                    }

                    console.log(`[INFO] Generation stream completely finished!`);
                    currentState = State.COMPLETED;
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log(`[ERROR] Generation Phase Error: ${e.message}`);
                    currentState = State.COMPLETED; 
                }
                break;

            case State.COMPLETED:
                console.log(`[STATE] âœ… ${currentState}: AI response finalized.`);
                console.log(`[WAITING] â³ Waiting 5 seconds before copying (as requested)...`);
                await page.waitForTimeout(5000); 
                currentState = State.OUTPUT_COPIED;
                break;

            case State.OUTPUT_COPIED: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸ“‹ ${currentState}: Extracting Text safely from DOM...`);
                
                currentOutput = await page.evaluate(() => {
                    const btns = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button')).filter(b => 
                        (b.getAttribute('aria-label') || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy') || 
                        (b.title || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy')
                    );
                    
                    const chatBtns = btns.filter(b => !b.closest('[role="dialog"]') && !b.closest('[class*="banner"]') && !b.closest('footer'));
                    if (chatBtns.length === 0) return "[ERROR] No valid copy button found in chat area.";

                    const targetBtn = chatBtns[chatBtns.length - 1]; 
                    
                    let container = targetBtn.closest('.group') || targetBtn.closest('[data-testid="message-row"]');
                    
                    if (!container) {
                        container = targetBtn.parentElement;
                        for(let i=0; i<4; i++) {
                            if(container.parentElement && container.parentElement.tagName !== 'BODY') {
                                container = container.parentElement;
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    const markdownDiv = container.querySelector('.prose, .markdown, [class*="prose"]');
                    if (markdownDiv) return markdownDiv.innerText.trim();

                    const clone = container.cloneNode(true);
                    clone.querySelectorAll('button, svg, img, [role="button"], a[href^="http"]').forEach(el => el.remove());
                    return clone.innerText.trim();
                });
                
                if (!currentOutput || currentOutput.trim() === "") {
                    currentOutput = "[ERROR] Extracted text was empty.";
                }
                
                currentState = State.OUTPUT_SAVED;
                break;

            case State.OUTPUT_SAVED: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸ’¾ ${currentState}: Saving to Master Outbox...`);
                
                const outboxData = {
                    task_id: currentTask.task_id,
                    sender: "@grok", 
                    receiver: "@administrator", // ðŸ”´ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿à¦•à§à¦Ÿà¦²à¦¿ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¡à¦®à¦¿à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦›à§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡
                    original_prompt: currentTask.prompt,
                    response: currentOutput,
                    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
                };

                const outboxFilePath = path.join(OUTBOX_DIR, `outbox_grok_${Date.now()}.json`);
                fs.writeFileSync(outboxFilePath, JSON.stringify(outboxData, null, 4));
                
                // ðŸ”´ à¦‡à¦¨à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦²à¦Ÿà¦¾ à¦¡à¦¿à¦²à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à¦¯à¦¼à¦¾ à¦¹à¦²à§‹
                if (fs.existsSync(INBOX_FILE)) {
                    fs.unlinkSync(INBOX_FILE);
                }
                
                console.log(`[SUCCESS] Task Delivered to Administrator! Returning to Listening Mode in 3 seconds...`);
                
                currentTask = null;
                currentOutput = "";
                
                await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
                currentState = State.CONVERSATION_READY; 
                break;
        }
    }
})();

// ========================================================
// ðŸ”´ Graceful Shutdown: 100% FIXED: à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬ à¦•à§‡à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦¬à¦¿à¦¦à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼ à¦¨à§‡à¦¬à§‡
// ========================================================
process.on('message', async (msg) => {
    if (msg === 'shutdown') {
        console.log(`[ðŸ›‘ SHUTDOWN] Closing Grok tab...`);
        
        setTimeout(() => { process.exit(0); }, 2500);

        try {
            if (typeof page !== 'undefined' && page && !page.isClosed()) {
                await page.close(); // ðŸ”´ à¦¶à§à¦§à§ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬ à¦•à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¬à§‡, à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦¨à¦¯à¦¼!
            }
            if (typeof browser !== 'undefined' && browser && typeof browser.disconnect === 'function') {
                await browser.disconnect(); 
            }
        } catch (err) {}
        
        process.exit(0); 
    }
});

                        browser = await chromium.connectOverCDP(endpoint);
                        context = browser.contexts()[0];
                        page = await context.newPage();
                        console.log(`[INFO] âœ… Connected! Opening Perplexity in a new tab...`);
                    } catch (cdpError) {
                        // à§¨. à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à§‡, à¦¤à¦¬à§‡ à¦¸à§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦‡ à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦‰à¦‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à§‹ à¦–à§à¦²à¦¬à§‡!
                        console.log(`[INFO] Browser not found. Launching new window for ${profileName}...`);
                        const ACCOUNTS_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'Accounts', profileName); 
                        if (!fs.existsSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR, { recursive: true });
                        
                        context = await chromium.launchPersistentContext(ACCOUNTS_DIR, {
                            headless: false,
                            channel: 'chrome',
                            viewport: null,
                            ignoreDefaultArgs: ["--enable-automation"], 
                            args: [
                                `--remote-debugging-port=${targetPort}`, 
                                '--start-maximized',
                                '--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled',
                                '--no-sandbox',
                                '--disable-infobars'
                            ]
                        });
                        let pages = context.pages();
                        page = pages.length > 0 ? pages[0] : await context.newPage();
                        console.log(`[INFO] âœ… New Browser Launched successfully on port ${targetPort}!`);
                    }

                    page.setDefaultTimeout(0); 
                    await page.goto('https://www.perplexity.ai/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
                    
                    await page.waitForTimeout(5000); 
                    currentState = State.CONVERSATION_READY;
                } catch (err) {
                    console.log(`[ERROR DETAILS] ${err.message}`); 
                    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 5000));
                }
                break;

            case State.CONVERSATION_READY: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸ’¬ ${currentState}: Handling popups and finding input box...`);
                
                try {
                    await page.evaluate(() => {
                        document.querySelectorAll('div[role="dialog"]').forEach(e => {
                            const btn = e.querySelector('button');
                            if(btn) btn.click();
                            else e.remove();
                        });
                    });
                } catch(e) {}

                await page.locator(INPUT_SELECTOR).last().waitFor({ state: 'attached', timeout: 30000 });
                currentState = State.LISTENING; 
                break;

            case State.LISTENING:
                // à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à¦¿ à§© à¦¸à§‡à¦•à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡ à¦ªà¦° à¦ªà¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦•à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§‹à¦²à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦‡à¦¨à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸ à¦šà§‡à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡
                await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
                
                try {
                    if (fs.existsSync(INBOX_FILE)) {
                        const rawData = fs.readFileSync(INBOX_FILE, 'utf8');
                        if (rawData.trim() !== "" && rawData.trim() !== "{}") {
                            const data = JSON.parse(rawData);
                            
                            // à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡ à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦®à§à¦ªà¦Ÿ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à§‡
                            if (data.prompt && data.task_id) {
                                console.log(`\n[STATE] ðŸ“¥ NEW TASK RECEIVED from ${data.sender}! Task ID: ${data.task_id}`);
                                currentTask = data;
                                currentState = State.TYPING;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    // JSON parsing error
                }
                break;

            case State.TYPING: 
                console.log(`[STATE] âŒ¨ï¸  ${currentState}: Blazing fast Copy-Paste typing...`);
                
                const promptBox = page.locator(INPUT_SELECTOR).last();
                // force: true à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¦£à§‡ à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸ à¦…à¦¦à§ƒà¦¶à§à¦¯ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦“ à¦¸à§‡ à¦œà§‹à¦° à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦‡à¦ª à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡
                await promptBox.click({ force: true });
                await page.waitForTimeout(300);
                
                await page.keyboard.press('Control+A');
                await page.keyboard.press('Backspace');
                await randomDelay(100, 200);
                
                // ðŸ”´ à¦«à¦¾à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦ªà¦¿-à¦ªà§‡à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦œà§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨
                await page.evaluate((text) => {
                    const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
                    textarea.value = text;
                    document.body.appendChild(textarea);
                    textarea.select();
                    document.execCommand('copy');
                    document.body.removeChild(textarea);
                }, currentTask.prompt);

                await promptBox.click({ force: true });
                await page.keyboard.press('Control+V'); // à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¤à§‹ à¦à¦• à¦¸à§‡à¦•à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦®à§à¦ªà¦Ÿ à¦ªà§‡à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ à¦¹à¦¯à¦¼à§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡!
                
                await randomDelay(800, 1500);
                currentState = State.PROMPT_SENT;
                break;

            // ðŸ”´ à¦à¦–à¦¾à¦¨ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦¦à§‡à¦“à¦¯à¦¼à¦¾ à¦¹à§à¦¬à¦¹à§ à¦²à¦œà¦¿à¦• à¦¶à§à¦°à§
            case State.PROMPT_SENT: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸš€ ${currentState}: Pressing 'Enter' to submit.`);
                await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
                currentState = State.GENERATING;
                break;

            case State.GENERATING: 
                console.log(`[STATE] âš™ï¸  ${currentState}: AI is searching & generating. Monitoring text stability...`);
                try {
                    let lastText = "";
                    let stableChecks = 0;
                    
                    while (stableChecks < 2) {
                        await page.waitForTimeout(2000); 
                        
                        const currentText = await page.evaluate(() => {
                            const msgs = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.prose, div[dir="auto"]'));
                            if(msgs.length === 0) return "";
                            return msgs[msgs.length - 1].innerText;
                        });

                        if (currentText === lastText && currentText.trim().length > 5) {
                            stableChecks++;
                        } else {
                            stableChecks = 0;
                            lastText = currentText;
                        }
                    }

                    console.log(`[INFO] Generation stream completely finished!`);
                    currentState = State.COMPLETED;
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log(`[ERROR] Generation Phase Error: ${e.message}`);
                    currentState = State.COMPLETED; 
                }
                break;

            case State.COMPLETED:
                console.log(`[STATE] âœ… ${currentState}: AI response finalized.`);
                console.log(`[WAITING] â³ Waiting 5 seconds before copying (allowing UI to fully render)...`);
                await page.waitForTimeout(5000); 
                currentState = State.OUTPUT_COPIED;
                break;

            case State.OUTPUT_COPIED: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸ“‹ ${currentState}: Extracting perfectly formatted text from DOM...`);
                
                currentOutput = await page.evaluate(() => {
                    const messages = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.prose, div[dir="auto"]'));
                    
                    if (messages.length > 0) {
                        const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
                        const clone = lastMessage.cloneNode(true);
                        
                        clone.querySelectorAll('.sr-only, button, svg, img, [role="button"], a.citation, a[href^="http"]').forEach(el => el.remove());
                        return clone.innerText.trim();
                    }

                    const btns = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button, div[role="button"]')).filter(b => 
                        (b.getAttribute('aria-label') || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy') || 
                        (b.title || '').toLowerCase().includes('copy')
                    );
                    
                    if(btns.length > 0) {
                        let container = btns[btns.length - 1].parentElement;
                        for(let i=0; i<4; i++) { if(container.parentElement && container.parentElement.tagName !== 'BODY') container = container.parentElement; }
                        const clone = container.cloneNode(true);
                        clone.querySelectorAll('.sr-only, button, svg, img, [role="button"], a.citation, a[href^="http"]').forEach(el => el.remove());
                        return clone.innerText.trim();
                    }

                    return "[ERROR] No valid Perplexity message found.";
                });
                
                if (!currentOutput || currentOutput.trim() === "") {
                    currentOutput = "[ERROR] Extracted text was empty.";
                }
                
                currentState = State.OUTPUT_SAVED;
                break;
            // ðŸ”´ à¦à¦–à¦¾à¦¨à§‡ à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦¦à§‡à¦“à¦¯à¦¼à¦¾ à¦¹à§à¦¬à¦¹à§ à¦²à¦œà¦¿à¦• à¦¶à§‡à¦·

            case State.OUTPUT_SAVED: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸ’¾ ${currentState}: Saving to Master Outbox...`);
                
                // ðŸ”´ à¦†à¦‰à¦Ÿà¦ªà§à¦Ÿ à¦¸à§‡à¦­ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° JSON à¦ªà¦¦à§à¦§à¦¤à¦¿
                const outboxData = {
                    task_id: currentTask.task_id,
                    sender: "@perplexity", 
                    receiver: "@administrator", // ðŸ”´ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿à¦•à§à¦Ÿà¦²à¦¿ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¡à¦®à¦¿à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦›à§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡
                    original_prompt: currentTask.prompt,
                    response: currentOutput,
                    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
                };

                const outboxFilePath = path.join(OUTBOX_DIR, `outbox_perplexity_${Date.now()}.json`);
                fs.writeFileSync(outboxFilePath, JSON.stringify(outboxData, null, 4));
                
                // ðŸ”´ à¦•à¦¾à¦œ à¦¶à§‡à¦·! à¦‡à¦¨à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦²à¦Ÿà¦¾ à¦¡à¦¿à¦²à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à¦¯à¦¼à¦¾ à¦¹à¦²à§‹
                if (fs.existsSync(INBOX_FILE)) {
                    fs.unlinkSync(INBOX_FILE);
                }
                
                console.log(`[SUCCESS] Task Delivered to Administrator! Returning to Listening Mode in 3 seconds...`);
                
                currentTask = null;
                currentOutput = "";
                
                await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
                currentState = State.CONVERSATION_READY; 
                break;
        }
    }
})();

// ========================================================
// ðŸ”´ Graceful Shutdown: 100% FIXED: à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬ à¦•à§‡à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦¬à¦¿à¦¦à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼ à¦¨à§‡à¦¬à§‡
// ========================================================
process.on('message', async (msg) => {
    if (msg === 'shutdown') {
        console.log(`[ðŸ›‘ SHUTDOWN] Closing Perplexity tab...`);
        
        // à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦†à¦ª à¦¸à§‡à¦«à¦Ÿà¦¿: à¦†à¦¡à¦¼à¦¾à¦‡ à¦¸à§‡à¦•à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡ à¦ªà¦° à¦¯à§‡à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦®à§‚à¦²à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¸à§‡à¦¸ à¦•à¦¿à¦² à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡
        setTimeout(() => { process.exit(0); }, 2500);

        try {
            if (typeof page !== 'undefined' && page && !page.isClosed()) {
                await page.close(); // ðŸ”´ à¦¶à§à¦§à§ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬ à¦•à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¬à§‡, à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦¨à¦¯à¦¼!
            }
            if (typeof browser !== 'undefined' && browser && typeof browser.disconnect === 'function') {
                await browser.disconnect(); 
            }
        } catch (err) {}
        
        process.exit(0); 
    }
});

/* === qwen_engine.js === */

const { chromium } = require('playwright');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// ðŸ”´ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦¨à¦¾à¦® à¦°à¦¿à¦¸à¦¿à¦­ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦•à§‹à¦¡ 
const profileName = process.argv[2] || 'Normal_Browser';

// --- Master Controller Paths ---
const MASTER_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'Master_Controller');
const INBOX_FILE = path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'Inbox', 'qwen_inbox.json');
const OUTBOX_DIR = path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'Outbox');

// ðŸ”´ à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦• à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§à¦¸ à§§: à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦«à§‹à¦²à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦° à¦ªà¦¾à¦¥
const ACCOUNTS_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'Accounts', profileName); 

// à¦«à§‹à¦²à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦°à¦—à§à¦²à§‹ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦²à§‡ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¨à§‡à¦¬à§‡
if (!fs.existsSync(OUTBOX_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(OUTBOX_DIR, { recursive: true });
if (!fs.existsSync(path.dirname(INBOX_FILE))) fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(INBOX_FILE), { recursive: true });
if (!fs.existsSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR, { recursive: true }); // à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦«à§‹à¦²à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦° à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿

const State = {
    INITIATING: 'Initiating System',
    CONVERSATION_READY: 'Conversation Ready',
    LISTENING: 'Listening for Tasks',
    TYPING: 'Typing',
    PROMPT_SENT: 'Prompt Sent',
    GENERATING: 'Generating',
    COMPLETED: 'Completed',
    OUTPUT_COPIED: 'Output Copied',
    OUTPUT_SAVED: 'Output Saved'
};

let currentState = State.INITIATING;
let browser, context, page;
let currentTask = null; 
let currentOutput = "";

const INPUT_SELECTOR = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'ai_selectors.json'))).qwen.chatBox; } catch(e) { return 'textarea, [contenteditable="true"]'; } })();
const randomDelay = (min, max) => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.random() * (max - min) + min));

(async () => {
    console.log(`\n======================================================`);
    console.log(` ðŸ‰ Qwen AI Worker Node [Profile: ${profileName}]`);
    console.log(`======================================================\n`);

    while (true) { 
        switch (currentState) {
            
            // ðŸ”´ 100% FIXED: SMART HYBRID MODE + ANTI-BOT BYPASS + DYNAMIC PORT
            case State.INITIATING:
                try {
                    // --- ðŸ”´ à¦†à¦²à¦¾à¦¦à¦¾ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦†à¦²à¦¾à¦¦à¦¾ à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿ à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦• ---
                    const PORTS_FILE = path.join(MASTER_DIR, 'active_ports.json');
                    let targetPort = 9222;
                    
                    try {
                        if (fs.existsSync(PORTS_FILE)) {
                            let portsData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(PORTS_FILE, 'utf8'));
                            if (portsData[profileName]) {
                                targetPort = portsData[profileName]; 
                            } else {
                                const usedPorts = Object.values(portsData);
                                targetPort = usedPorts.length > 0 ? Math.max(...usedPorts) + 1 : 9222;
                                portsData[profileName] = targetPort;
                                fs.writeFileSync(PORTS_FILE, JSON.stringify(portsData, null, 4));
                            }
                        } else {
                            fs.writeFileSync(PORTS_FILE, JSON.stringify({ [profileName]: 9222 }, null, 4));
                        }
                    } catch(e) {}

                    const endpoint = `http://127.0.0.1:${targetPort}`;
                    
                    try {
                        // à§§. à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¥à¦®à§‡ à¦šà§‡à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿ ${targetPort} à¦¤à§‡ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¨ à¦†à¦›à§‡ à¦•à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾
                        console.log(`[STATE] ðŸŒ Checking for Browser on Port ${targetPort} (Profile: ${profileName})...`);
                        browser = await chromium.connectOverCDP(endpoint);
                        context = browser.contexts()[0];
                        page = await context.newPage();
                        console.log(`[INFO] âœ… Connected! Opening Qwen in a new tab...`);
                    } catch (cdpError) {
                        // à§¨. à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à§‡, à¦¤à¦¬à§‡ à¦¸à§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦‡ à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦‰à¦‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à§‹ à¦–à§à¦²à¦¬à§‡!
                        console.log(`[INFO] Browser not found. Launching new window for ${profileName}...`);
                        const ACCOUNTS_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'Accounts', profileName); 
                        if (!fs.existsSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(ACCOUNTS_DIR, { recursive: true });
                        
                        context = await chromium.launchPersistentContext(ACCOUNTS_DIR, {
                            headless: false,
                            channel: 'chrome',
                            viewport: null,
                            ignoreDefaultArgs: ["--enable-automation"], 
                            args: [
                                `--remote-debugging-port=${targetPort}`, 
                                '--start-maximized',
                                '--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled',
                                '--no-sandbox',
                                '--disable-infobars'
                            ]
                        });
                        let pages = context.pages();
                        page = pages.length > 0 ? pages[0] : await context.newPage();
                        console.log(`[INFO] âœ… New Browser Launched successfully on port ${targetPort}!`);
                    }

                    page.setDefaultTimeout(0); 
                    await page.goto('https://chat.qwenlm.ai/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
                    
                    await page.waitForTimeout(5000); 
                    currentState = State.CONVERSATION_READY;
                } catch (err) {
                    console.log(`[ERROR DETAILS] ${err.message}`); 
                    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 5000));
                }
                break;

            case State.CONVERSATION_READY: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸ’¬ ${currentState}: Handling popups and finding input box...`);
                
                try {
                    await page.evaluate(() => {
                        document.querySelectorAll('div[role="dialog"]').forEach(e => {
                            const btn = e.querySelector('button');
                            if(btn) btn.click();
                            else e.remove();
                        });
                    });
                } catch(e) {}

                await page.waitForFunction(() => {
                    return document.querySelectorAll('textarea, [contenteditable="true"]').length > 0;
                }, { timeout: 30000 });
                
                currentState = State.LISTENING; 
                break;

            case State.LISTENING:
                await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
                
                try {
                    if (fs.existsSync(INBOX_FILE)) {
                        const rawData = fs.readFileSync(INBOX_FILE, 'utf8');
                        if (rawData.trim() !== "" && rawData.trim() !== "{}") {
                            const data = JSON.parse(rawData);
                            
                            if (data.prompt && data.task_id) {
                                console.log(`\n[STATE] ðŸ“¥ NEW TASK RECEIVED from ${data.sender}! Task ID: ${data.task_id}`);
                                currentTask = data;
                                currentState = State.TYPING;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    // JSON parsing error
                }
                break;

            case State.TYPING: 
                console.log(`[STATE] âŒ¨ï¸  ${currentState}: Blazing fast Copy-Paste typing...`);
                
                await page.evaluate(() => {
                    const boxes = document.querySelectorAll('textarea, [contenteditable="true"]');
                    if(boxes.length > 0) boxes[boxes.length - 1].focus();
                });

                const promptBox = page.locator(INPUT_SELECTOR).last();
                await promptBox.click({ force: true });
                await page.waitForTimeout(300);
                
                await page.keyboard.press('Control+A');
                await page.keyboard.press('Backspace');
                await randomDelay(100, 200);
                
                await page.evaluate((text) => {
                    const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
                    textarea.value = text;
                    document.body.appendChild(textarea);
                    textarea.select();
                    document.execCommand('copy');
                    document.body.removeChild(textarea);
                }, currentTask.prompt);

                await promptBox.click({ force: true });
                await page.keyboard.press('Control+V'); 
                
                await randomDelay(800, 1500);
                currentState = State.PROMPT_SENT;
                break;

            case State.PROMPT_SENT: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸš€ ${currentState}: Pressing 'Enter' to submit.`);
                await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
                currentState = State.GENERATING;
                break;

            case State.GENERATING: 
                console.log(`[STATE] âš™ï¸  ${currentState}: AI is generating. Monitoring entire page stability...`);
                try {
                    let lastText = "";
                    let stableChecks = 0;
                    
                    while (stableChecks < 10) { 
                        await page.waitForTimeout(2000); 
                        const currentText = await page.evaluate(() => document.body.innerText);

                        if (currentText === lastText && currentText.trim().length > 50) {
                            stableChecks++;
                            console.log(`[WAITING] No new text detected. Wait count: ${stableChecks}/10`);
                        } else {
                            stableChecks = 0;
                            lastText = currentText;
                        }
                    }

                    console.log(`[INFO] Generation stream completely finished!`);
                    currentState = State.COMPLETED;
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log(`[ERROR] Generation Phase Error: ${e.message}`);
                    currentState = State.COMPLETED; 
                }
                break;

            case State.COMPLETED:
                console.log(`[STATE] âœ… ${currentState}: AI response finalized.`);
                console.log(`[WAITING] â³ Waiting 5 seconds before copying (allowing UI to fully render)...`);
                await page.waitForTimeout(5000); 
                currentState = State.OUTPUT_COPIED;
                break;

            case State.OUTPUT_COPIED: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸ“‹ ${currentState}: Extracting text safely...`);
                
                try {
                    const isCopied = await page.evaluate(async () => {
                        const copyBtns = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button, svg, div[role="button"]')).filter(b => {
                            const aria = (b.getAttribute('aria-label') || '').toLowerCase();
                            const title = (b.title || '').toLowerCase();
                            const cls = (b.getAttribute('class') || '').toLowerCase(); 
                            return aria.includes('copy') || title.includes('copy') || cls.includes('copy');
                        });

                        if (copyBtns.length > 0) {
                            copyBtns[copyBtns.length - 1].click();
                            return true;
                        }
                        return false;
                    });

                    if (isCopied) {
                        await page.waitForTimeout(1000); 
                        currentOutput = await page.evaluate(async () => {
                            try {
                                return await navigator.clipboard.readText();
                            } catch (e) {
                                return "";
                            }
                        });
                    }

                    if (!currentOutput || currentOutput.trim() === "") {
                        console.log(`[INFO] Copy button failed. Using fallback DOM extractor...`);
                        
                        currentOutput = await page.evaluate(() => {
                            const containers = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.markdown-body, .prose, .markdown, [class*="markdown"]'));
                            
                            if (containers.length > 0) {
                                const mainContainers = containers.filter(el => {
                                    let parent = el.parentElement;
                                    while (parent && parent.tagName !== 'BODY') {
                                        const pClass = (parent.getAttribute('class') || '').toLowerCase(); 
                                        if (pClass.includes('markdown-body') || pClass.includes('prose') || pClass.includes('markdown')) {
                                            return false;
                                        }
                                        parent = parent.parentElement;
                                    }
                                    return true; 
                                });

                                if (mainContainers.length > 0) {
                                    const lastMessage = mainContainers[mainContainers.length - 1]; 
                                    const clone = lastMessage.cloneNode(true);
                                    
                                    const garbage = clone.querySelectorAll('.sr-only, button, svg, img, [role="button"], details, style, script');
                                    for (let i = 0; i < garbage.length; i++) {
                                        if (garbage[i] && garbage[i].parentNode) {
                                            garbage[i].parentNode.removeChild(garbage[i]); 
                                        }
                                    }
                                    return clone.innerText.trim();
                                }
                            }
                            return "[ERROR] No valid Qwen message found.";
                        });
                    }

                    currentOutput = currentOutput.replace(/^Thinking completed.*?[\r\n]+/i, '');
                    currentOutput = currentOutput.replace(/^Thinking.*?[\r\n]+/i, '');
                    currentOutput = currentOutput.replace(/AI-generated content may not be accurate.*/gi, '');
                    currentOutput = currentOutput.trim();

                } catch (e) {
                    currentOutput = "[ERROR] Extraction failed: " + e.message;
                }
                
                if (!currentOutput || currentOutput === "") {
                    currentOutput = "[ERROR] Extracted text was empty.";
                }
                
                currentState = State.OUTPUT_SAVED;
                break;

            case State.OUTPUT_SAVED: 
                console.log(`[STATE] ðŸ’¾ ${currentState}: Saving to Master Outbox...`);
                
                const outboxData = {
                    task_id: currentTask.task_id,
                    sender: "@qwen", 
                    receiver: "@administrator", // ðŸ”´ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿à¦•à§à¦Ÿà¦²à¦¿ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¡à¦®à¦¿à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦›à§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡
                    original_prompt: currentTask.prompt,
                    response: currentOutput,
                    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
                };

                const outboxFilePath = path.join(OUTBOX_DIR, `outbox_qwen_${Date.now()}.json`);
                fs.writeFileSync(outboxFilePath, JSON.stringify(outboxData, null, 4));
                
                // ðŸ”´ à¦•à¦¾à¦œ à¦¶à§‡à¦·! à¦‡à¦¨à¦¬à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦²à¦Ÿà¦¾ à¦¡à¦¿à¦²à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à¦¯à¦¼à¦¾ à¦¹à¦²à§‹
                if (fs.existsSync(INBOX_FILE)) {
                    fs.unlinkSync(INBOX_FILE);
                }
                
                console.log(`[SUCCESS] Task Delivered to Administrator! Returning to Listening Mode in 3 seconds...`);
                
                currentTask = null;
                currentOutput = "";
                
                await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
                currentState = State.CONVERSATION_READY; 
                break;
        }
    }
})();

// ========================================================
// ðŸ”´ Graceful Shutdown: 100% FIXED: à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬ à¦•à§‡à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦¬à¦¿à¦¦à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼ à¦¨à§‡à¦¬à§‡
// ========================================================
process.on('message', async (msg) => {
    if (msg === 'shutdown') {
        console.log(`[ðŸ›‘ SHUTDOWN] Closing Qwen tab...`);
        
        setTimeout(() => { process.exit(0); }, 2500);

        try {
            if (typeof page !== 'undefined' && page && !page.isClosed()) {
                await page.close(); // ðŸ”´ à¦¶à§à¦§à§ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬ à¦•à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¬à§‡, à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦¨à¦¯à¦¼!
            }
            if (typeof browser !== 'undefined' && browser && typeof browser.disconnect === 'function') {
                await browser.disconnect(); 
            }
        } catch (err) {}
        
        process.exit(0); 
    }
});

        <!-- How it works -->
        <h4 style="color: #fff; margin-bottom: 8px; font-size: 14px;">ðŸ—ï¸ AI Roles & Architecture</h4>
        <p style="margin-bottom: 12px;">This application uses a strict <b>Tag Routing System</b>. The AIs have specific
          rules on who they can communicate with:</p>
        <ul style="padding-left: 20px; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 15px;">
          <li><b style="color: #ffb74d;">@administrator</b>: The orchestrator. It manages tasks, decides which worker to
            use, and sends the final output to the Editor.</li>
          <li><b style="color: #64b5f6;">Worker AIs (@chatgpt, @gemini, etc)</b>: They do the hard work. They can ONLY
            communicate back to the <code>@administrator</code>.</li>
          <li><b style="color: #e91e63;">@final</b>: The Editor. It reviews the Administrator's compiled data, formats
            it perfectly, and outputs it to you.</li>
        </ul>

        <!-- System Recording -->
        <h4 style="color: #e57373; margin-bottom: 8px; font-size: 14px;">ðŸŽ™ï¸ "System Recording" Switch</h4>
        <p style="margin-bottom: 8px;">To prevent AIs from talking to each other automatically before they know their
          jobs, use this switch:</p>
        <ul style="padding-left: 20px; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 15px;">
          <li style="margin-bottom: 6px;"><strong>ðŸ”´ OFF (Warm-up Mode):</strong> The Master Router pauses. Your
            messages are sent directly to the selected AI, but their responses are <b>ignored</b> by the system. Use
            this to train them!</li>
          <li><strong>ðŸŸ¢ ON (Active Routing):</strong> The loop starts. If an AI uses a tag (e.g.,
            <code>@chatgpt</code>), the message is instantly routed to that AI's inbox automatically.
          </li>
        </ul>

        <!-- Training Guide -->
        <h4 style="color: #4CAF50; margin-bottom: 8px; font-size: 14px;">ðŸ§  How to Train Your AIs (Step-by-Step)</h4>
        <div
          style="background: #141418; padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px left solid #4CAF50; margin-bottom: 12px;">
          <ol style="padding-left: 20px; margin: 0; color: #d0d0d0;">
            <li style="margin-bottom: 8px;">Turn <b>System Recording OFF</b>.</li>
            <li style="margin-bottom: 8px;">Select <b>Administrator</b> and send its master prompt (e.g., "Act as the
              Master Orchestrator..."). Wait for it to confirm.</li>
            <li style="margin-bottom: 8px;">Select <b>Final</b> and send its formatting rules (e.g., "Act as the Editor.
              Always end with @admin..."). Wait for it to confirm.</li>
            <li>Turn <b>System Recording ON</b>. Now give your actual task to the Administrator!</li>
          </ol>
        </div>

  <!-- CINEMATIC BOOT SEQUENCE JS -->
  <script>
    (function () {
      var boot = document.getElementById('bootSequence');
      if (!boot) return;

      var alreadyBooted = false;
      try { alreadyBooted = sessionStorage.getItem('cepBooted') === '1'; } catch (e) { }
      if (alreadyBooted) { boot.remove(); return; }

      var reduced = false;
      try { reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; } catch (e) { }

      var logEl = document.getElementById('bootLog');
      var fillEl = document.getElementById('bootProgressFill');
      var pctEl = document.getElementById('bootPercent');
      var statusEl = document.getElementById('bootStatus');
      var sysDots = boot.querySelectorAll('.boot-sys-dot');
      var particleField = document.getElementById('bootParticles');

      var logs = [
        'Initializing neural core',
        'Loading creative modules',
        'Connecting AI systems',
        'Calibrating workflows',
        'Systems online'
      ];

      if (!reduced) {
        var count = 46;
        var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
          var p = document.createElement('span');
          p.className = 'boot-particle';
          p.style.left = (Math.random() * 100) + '%';
          p.style.top = (55 + Math.random() * 45) + '%';
          p.style.setProperty('--size', (1 + Math.random() * 1.8).toFixed(1) + 'px');
          p.style.setProperty('--dur', (7 + Math.random() * 9).toFixed(2) + 's');
          p.style.setProperty('--delay', (Math.random() * 8).toFixed(2) + 's');
          p.style.setProperty('--drift', (Math.random() * 24 - 12).toFixed(0) + 'px');
          frag.appendChild(p);
        }
        particleField.appendChild(frag);
      }

      var finished = false;
      function finishBoot() {
        if (finished) return;
        finished = true;
        try { sessionStorage.setItem('cepBooted', '1'); } catch (e) { }
        boot.classList.add('boot-sequence--exit');
        setTimeout(function () {
          if (boot && boot.parentNode) boot.remove();
        }, 1000);
      }

      var step = -1;
      function setStep(index) {
        if (index === step) return;
        step = index;
        logEl.textContent = logs[step];
        logEl.classList.remove('boot-log--flicker');
        void logEl.offsetWidth;
        logEl.classList.add('boot-log--flicker');
      }

      var totalDuration = reduced ? 250 : 3200;
      var consoleStartDelay = reduced ? 0 : 2500;
      var startTime = null;

      function tick(now) {
        if (startTime === null) startTime = now;
        var elapsed = now - startTime;
        var pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((elapsed / totalDuration) * 100));

        fillEl.style.width = pct + '%';
        pctEl.textContent = pct + '%';

        var logIndex = Math.min(logs.length - 1, Math.floor((pct / 100) * logs.length));
        setStep(logIndex);

        var liveCount = Math.round((pct / 100) * sysDots.length);
        for (var i = 0; i < sysDots.length; i++) {
          sysDots[i].classList.toggle('is-live', i < liveCount);
        }

        if (pct < 100) {
          requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        } else {
          statusEl.textContent = 'Online';
          statusEl.classList.add('boot-status--online');
          setTimeout(finishBoot, reduced ? 150 : 550);
        }
      }

      setTimeout(function () { requestAnimationFrame(tick); }, consoleStartDelay);

      function skip() {
        boot.removeEventListener('click', skip);
        document.removeEventListener('keydown', skip);
        finishBoot();
      }
      boot.addEventListener('click', skip);
      document.addEventListener('keydown', skip);

      setTimeout(finishBoot, 5000);
    })();
  </script>
  <script src="ui_controller.js"></script>
</body>

</html>

<script>
(function () {
  var boot = document.getElementById('bootSequence');
  if (!boot) return;

  // Only play the full cinematic boot once per app session.
  var alreadyBooted = false;
  try { alreadyBooted = sessionStorage.getItem('cepBooted') === '1'; } catch (e) {}
  if (alreadyBooted) { boot.remove(); return; }

  var reduced = false;
  try { reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; } catch (e) {}

  var logEl = document.getElementById('bootLog');
  var fillEl = document.getElementById('bootProgressFill');
  var pctEl = document.getElementById('bootPercent');
  var statusEl = document.getElementById('bootStatus');
  var sysDots = boot.querySelectorAll('.boot-sys-dot');
  var particleField = document.getElementById('bootParticles');

  var logs = [
    'Initializing neural core',
    'Loading creative modules',
    'Connecting AI systems',
    'Calibrating workflows',
    'Systems online'
  ];

  // Ambient particles (skipped under reduced motion).
  if (!reduced) {
    var count = 46;
    var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      var p = document.createElement('span');
      p.className = 'boot-particle';
      p.style.left = (Math.random() * 100) + '%';
      p.style.top = (55 + Math.random() * 45) + '%';
      p.style.setProperty('--size', (1 + Math.random() * 1.8).toFixed(1) + 'px');
      p.style.setProperty('--dur', (7 + Math.random() * 9).toFixed(2) + 's');
      p.style.setProperty('--delay', (Math.random() * 8).toFixed(2) + 's');
      p.style.setProperty('--drift', (Math.random() * 24 - 12).toFixed(0) + 'px');
      frag.appendChild(p);
    }
    particleField.appendChild(frag);
  }

  var finished = false;
  function finishBoot() {
    if (finished) return;
    finished = true;
    try { sessionStorage.setItem('cepBooted', '1'); } catch (e) {}
    boot.classList.add('boot-sequence--exit');
    setTimeout(function () {
      if (boot && boot.parentNode) boot.remove();
    }, 1000);
  }

  var step = -1;
  function setStep(index) {
    if (index === step) return;
    step = index;
    logEl.textContent = logs[step];
    logEl.classList.remove('boot-log--flicker');
    void logEl.offsetWidth;
    logEl.classList.add('boot-log--flicker');
  }

  var totalDuration = reduced ? 250 : 3200;
  var consoleStartDelay = reduced ? 0 : 2500; // matches .boot-console animation-delay
  var startTime = null;

  function tick(now) {
    if (startTime === null) startTime = now;
    var elapsed = now - startTime;
    var pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((elapsed / totalDuration) * 100));

    fillEl.style.width = pct + '%';
    pctEl.textContent = pct + '%';

    var logIndex = Math.min(logs.length - 1, Math.floor((pct / 100) * logs.length));
    setStep(logIndex);

    var liveCount = Math.round((pct / 100) * sysDots.length);
    for (var i = 0; i < sysDots.length; i++) {
      sysDots[i].classList.toggle('is-live', i < liveCount);
    }

    if (pct < 100) {
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    } else {
      statusEl.textContent = 'Online';
      statusEl.classList.add('boot-status--online');
      setTimeout(finishBoot, reduced ? 150 : 550);
    }
  }

  setTimeout(function () { requestAnimationFrame(tick); }, consoleStartDelay);

  // Let impatient users skip straight to the dashboard.
  function skip() {
    boot.removeEventListener('click', skip);
    document.removeEventListener('keydown', skip);
    finishBoot();
  }
  boot.addEventListener('click', skip);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', skip);

  // Safety net in case something above throws or stalls.
  setTimeout(finishBoot, 9000);
})();
</script>

// ============ Greeting ============
(function setGreeting() {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  document.getElementById('greeting').textContent = hour < 12 ? 'Good morning.' : hour < 18 ? 'Good afternoon.' : 'Good evening.';
})();

// ============ Sidebar collapse ============
(function setupSidebar() {
  const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
  document.getElementById('collapseBtn').addEventListener('click', () => sidebar.classList.toggle('collapsed'));
})();

// ============ Popovers ============
(function setupPopovers() {
  function wire(btnId, popId) {
    const btn = document.getElementById(btnId);
    const pop = document.getElementById(popId);
    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      document.querySelectorAll('.popover').forEach((p) => { if (p !== pop) p.classList.remove('open'); });
      pop.classList.toggle('open');
    });
  }
  wire('notifBtn', 'notifPopover');
  wire('profileBtn', 'profilePopover');
  document.addEventListener('click', () => document.querySelectorAll('.popover').forEach((p) => p.classList.remove('open')));
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') document.querySelectorAll('.popover').forEach((p) => p.classList.remove('open')); });
})();

// ============ Count-up (stat values), driven by the exact locked easing curve ============
(function setupCountUp() {
  const reduceMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  function cubicBezier(x1, y1, x2, y2) {
    function bx(t) { return 3*(1-t)*(1-t)*t*x1 + 3*(1-t)*t*t*x2 + t*t*t; }
    function by(t) { return 3*(1-t)*(1-t)*t*y1 + 3*(1-t)*t*t*y2 + t*t*t; }
    return function (x) {
      let t = x;
      for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        const dx = bx(t) - x;
        if (Math.abs(dx) < 0.001) break;
        const d = 3*(1-t)*(1-t)*x1 + 6*(1-t)*t*(x2-x1) + 3*t*t*(1-x2);
        if (Math.abs(d) < 1e-6) break;
        t -= dx / d;
      }
      return by(t);
    };
  }
  const easeStandard = cubicBezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);

  function animateCountUp(el, duration) {
    const target = parseInt(el.dataset.countTo, 10) || 0;
    if (reduceMotion) { el.textContent = target.toLocaleString(); return; }
    const start = performance.now();
    function tick(now) {
      const t = Math.min((now - start) / duration, 1);
      el.textContent = Math.round(easeStandard(t) * target).toLocaleString();
      if (t < 1) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      else el.textContent = target.toLocaleString();
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-count-to]').forEach((el, i) => {
    setTimeout(() => animateCountUp(el, 900), 260 + i * 80);
  });
})();

// ============ Material Presence â€” cursor-reactive surface system ============
// Full tier (nav items, module cards, primary button): position-aware light
// response + existing elevation/border shift. Module cards additionally get a
// restrained perspective tilt (~1.5Â° max), hover-only, from the same cursor
// position data â€” no separate tracking needed.
// Whisper tier (stat cards, activity/status rows): position-aware light only,
// at lower opacity, with no elevation change â€” present, never implying
// clickability.
// Atmosphere: the hero's primary ambient glow carries a slow, heavily-lagged
// bias toward general cursor activity, layered on top of its own independent
// idle breathing (pure CSS, always running).
// Everything here is skipped under prefers-reduced-motion; hover/focus states
// defined in CSS remain untouched.
(function initMaterialPresence() {
  const reduceMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  if (reduceMotion) return;

  let pointerX = window.innerWidth / 2;
  let pointerY = window.innerHeight / 2;
  let ticking = false;

  const proximityRadius = 48;
  const els = document.querySelectorAll('.material-react');
  const sidebarEl = document.getElementById('sidebar');

  function update() {
    if (sidebarEl) {
      const sRect = sidebarEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (pointerY >= sRect.top && pointerY <= sRect.bottom && pointerX >= sRect.left && pointerX <= sRect.right) {
        sidebarEl.style.setProperty('--sidebar-light-y', (((pointerY - sRect.top) / sRect.height) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%');
      }
    }

    els.forEach((el) => {
      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const nx = Math.max(rect.left, Math.min(pointerX, rect.right));
      const ny = Math.max(rect.top, Math.min(pointerY, rect.bottom));
      const dist = Math.hypot(pointerX - nx, pointerY - ny);
      const near = dist < proximityRadius;
      el.classList.toggle('is-near', near);

      const withinBounds = pointerX >= rect.left && pointerX <= rect.right && pointerY >= rect.top && pointerY <= rect.bottom;

      if (near || withinBounds) {
        const mx = ((pointerX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 100;
        const my = ((pointerY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 100;
        el.style.setProperty('--mx', mx.toFixed(1) + '%');
        el.style.setProperty('--my', my.toFixed(1) + '%');
      }

      if (el.classList.contains('tilt')) {
        const maxTilt = el.classList.contains('whisper') ? 1.6 : 3;
        if (withinBounds) {
          const mx = (pointerX - rect.left) / rect.width;
          const my = (pointerY - rect.top) / rect.height;
          el.style.setProperty('--tilt-x', ((0.5 - my) * maxTilt).toFixed(2) + 'deg');
          el.style.setProperty('--tilt-y', ((mx - 0.5) * maxTilt).toFixed(2) + 'deg');
        } else {
          el.style.setProperty('--tilt-x', '0deg');
          el.style.setProperty('--tilt-y', '0deg');
        }
      }
    });
    ticking = false;
  }

  document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    pointerX = e.clientX; pointerY = e.clientY;
    if (!ticking) { requestAnimationFrame(update); ticking = true; }
  }, { passive: true });

  const glow = document.getElementById('ambientGlow');
  if (glow) {
    let driftX = 0, driftY = 0;
    const maxDriftX = 50, maxDriftY = 20;
    (function driftLoop() {
      const targetX = ((pointerX / window.innerWidth) - 0.5) * 2 * maxDriftX;
      const targetY = ((pointerY / window.innerHeight) - 0.5) * 2 * maxDriftY;
      driftX += (targetX - driftX) * 0.004;
      driftY += (targetY - driftY) * 0.004;
      glow.style.transform = `translate(${driftX.toFixed(2)}px, ${driftY.toFixed(2)}px)`;
      requestAnimationFrame(driftLoop);
    })();
  }
})();
</script>

// à§§. ELECTRON SAFE LOADER (CRASH PROOF)
// ==========================================
let ipcRenderer = null;
try {
    if (typeof require !== 'undefined') {
        ipcRenderer = require('electron').ipcRenderer;
    } else {
        console.error("âš ï¸ WARNING: require() is blocked by Electron Security!");
    }
} catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
}

// ==========================================
// à§¨. GLOBAL SPA ROUTER & UTILS 
// ==========================================
window.switchPage = function (pageId, navElement, fallbackTitle) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.app-page').forEach(page => {
        page.style.display = 'none';
        page.classList.remove('active-page');
    });

    const targetPage = document.getElementById(pageId);
    if (targetPage) {
        targetPage.style.display = 'block';
        void targetPage.offsetWidth;
        targetPage.classList.add('active-page');
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-nav .nav-item').forEach(nav => nav.classList.remove('nav-item--active'));

    let title = fallbackTitle || "Dashboard";
    if (navElement) {
        navElement.classList.add('nav-item--active');
        const label = navElement.querySelector('.nav-label');
        if (label) title = label.textContent;
    } else {
        const mapping = { 'page-dashboard': 0, 'page-gemini': 1, 'page-flow': 2, 'page-workspace': 3 };
        const items = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-nav .nav-item');
        if (items[mapping[pageId]]) {
            items[mapping[pageId]].classList.add('nav-item--active');
            title = items[mapping[pageId]].querySelector('.nav-label').textContent;
        }
    }

    const topbarTitle = document.getElementById('topbar-page-title');
    if (topbarTitle) topbarTitle.textContent = title;

    if (typeof window.runCountUp === 'function') window.runCountUp();
};

const TAG_LABELS = { connect: 'CONNECT', ready: 'READY', loaded: 'LOADED', sent: 'SENT', generating: 'RUNNING', completed: 'DONE', waiting: 'WAIT', progress: 'SYNC', info: 'INFO', error: 'ERROR' };

function nowStamp(offsetMs) {
    const d = new Date(Date.now() - (offsetMs || 0));
    return d.toTimeString().slice(0, 8);
}

function createConsoleLogger(panelId) {
    return function (type, text, timeOverride) {
        const panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
        if (!panel) return;
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'console-line';
        const tag = TAG_LABELS[type] || type.toUpperCase();
        row.innerHTML = `<span class="console-time">${timeOverride || nowStamp()}</span><span class="console-tag ${type}">${tag}</span><span class="console-text">${text}</span>`;
        panel.appendChild(row);
        panel.scrollTop = panel.scrollHeight;
        while (panel.children.length > 60) panel.removeChild(panel.firstChild);
    }
}

window.logLine = createConsoleLogger('workspace-consolePanel');
window.geminiLogLine = createConsoleLogger('gemini-consolePanel');
window.flowLogLine = createConsoleLogger('flow-consolePanel');

// ==========================================
// à§¨.à§« LIVE DASHBOARD CONTROLLER
// ==========================================
window.LiveStats = { sessions: 0, images: 0, videos: 0, workflows: 0 };

window.updateDashboardStat = function(statName, value, deltaText, deltaClass) {
    const el = document.getElementById(`dash-stat-${statName}`);
    const deltaEl = document.getElementById(`dash-delta-${statName}`);
    if(el) { el.dataset.countTo = value; el.textContent = value; }
    if(deltaEl && deltaText) { deltaEl.textContent = deltaText; if(deltaClass) deltaEl.className = `stat-delta ${deltaClass}`; }
};

window.addRecentActivity = function(sysName, text, colorVar) {
    const list = document.getElementById('dash-activity-list');
    if(!list) return;
    const empty = list.querySelector('.popover-empty');
    if(empty) empty.remove();
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'activity-row material-react whisper';
    row.innerHTML = `<span class="system-dot" style="background: var(--${colorVar});"></span><div class="activity-body"><div class="activity-system" style="color: var(--${colorVar});">${sysName}</div><div class="activity-text">${text.replace(/<[^>]*>?/gm, '')}</div></div><span class="activity-time">${nowStamp()}</span>`;
    list.prepend(row);
    while(list.children.length > 8) list.removeChild(list.lastChild);
};

window.updateDashboardSystemStatus = function(id, isConnected) {
    const row = document.getElementById(`dash-status-${id}`);
    if(!row) return;
    const dot = row.querySelector('.system-dot');
    const pill = row.querySelector('.status-pill');
    if(isConnected) {
        dot.classList.add('live'); pill.textContent = 'Connected'; pill.className = 'status-pill connected';
    } else {
        dot.classList.remove('live'); pill.textContent = 'Idle'; pill.className = 'status-pill idle';
    }
    const activeCount = document.querySelectorAll('#page-dashboard .status-pill.connected').length;
    window.updateDashboardStat('sessions', activeCount, activeCount > 0 ? `${activeCount} Systems Online` : 'All Offline', activeCount > 0 ? 'positive' : 'neutral');
};

// ==========================================
// à§©. DOM INITIALIZATION (Buttons & Modals)
// ==========================================
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

    const hour = new Date().getHours();
